-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `device_response_logs`
--

CREATE TABLE `device_response_logs` (
  `response_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `device_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `room_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `trigger_type` enum('fingerprint','remote_pwa','doorbell','emergency_button') NOT NULL,
  `response_time_ms` smallint(5) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `action_performed` enum('lock','unlock','doorbell_alert','siren') NOT NULL,
  `success` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `notes` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `recorded_at` timestamp(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(3)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `doorbell_events`
--

CREATE TABLE `doorbell_events` (
  `event_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `room_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `device_id` int(10) UNSIGNED DEFAULT NULL,
  `buzzer_triggered` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `pwa_notified` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `notified_user_id` int(10) UNSIGNED DEFAULT NULL,
  `visitor_note` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `response_action` enum('admitted','denied','no_response','messaged') DEFAULT NULL,
  `responded_by` int(10) UNSIGNED DEFAULT NULL,
  `pressed_at` timestamp(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(3),
  `responded_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `fingerprints`
--

CREATE TABLE `fingerprints` (
  `fp_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `user_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `room_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `finger_label` varchar(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Right Index',
  `template_slot` tinyint(3) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `enrolled_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `enrolled_by` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `passkeys`
--

CREATE TABLE `passkeys` (
  `id` bigint(20) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `user_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `credential_id` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `credential` text NOT NULL,
  `name` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `last_used_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

--
-- Dumping data for table `passkeys`
--

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `rooms`
--

CREATE TABLE `rooms` (
  `room_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `room_code` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `room_name` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `location` varchar(150) DEFAULT NULL,
  `description` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_air_conditioned` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `capacity` smallint(5) UNSIGNED DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `door_state` enum('locked','unlocked') DEFAULT 'locked',
  `enrollment_mode` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `enroll_user_id` int(10) UNSIGNED DEFAULT NULL,
  `enroll_fp_id` tinyint(3) UNSIGNED DEFAULT NULL,
  `delete_mode` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `delete_fp_id` tinyint(3) UNSIGNED DEFAULT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

--
-- Dumping data for table `rooms`
--

INSERT INTO `rooms` (`room_id`, `room_code`, `room_name`, `location`, `description`, `is_air_conditioned`, `capacity`, `is_active`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `door_state`, `enrollment_mode`, `enroll_user_id`, `enroll_fp_id`, `delete_mode`, `delete_fp_id`) VALUES

-- Table structure for table `room_assignments`
--

CREATE TABLE `room_assignments` (
  `assignment_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `user_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `room_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `assigned_by` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `access_level` enum('full','schedule_only','readonly') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'full',
  `pin_code` varchar(4) DEFAULT NULL,
  `valid_from` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `valid_until` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `assigned_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `notes` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

--
-- Dumping data for table `room_assignments`
--

INSERT INTO `room_assignments` (`assignment_id`, `user_id`, `room_id`, `assigned_by`, `access_level`, `pin_code`, `valid_from`, `valid_until`, `is_active`, `assigned_at`, `notes`, `updated_at`) VALUES

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `sessions`
--

CREATE TABLE `sessions` (
  `id` varchar(128) NOT NULL,
  `user_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `ip_address` varchar(45) DEFAULT NULL,
  `user_agent` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `payload` longtext NOT NULL,
  `last_activity` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `last_active_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `expires_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp()
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `users`
--

CREATE TABLE `users` (
  `user_id` int(10) UNSIGNED NOT NULL,
  `full_name` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `email` varchar(150) NOT NULL,
  `contact_number` varchar(20) DEFAULT NULL,
  `birthdate` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `password` varchar(255) NOT NULL COMMENT 'Laravel Hashed Password',
  `verification_code` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'Hashed OTP for email verification',
  `email_verified_at` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `role` enum('Dean','Admin','Staff') NOT NULL,
  `auth_code` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'Hashed 6-digit PIN for door override',
  `profile_photo` varchar(255) DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `remember_token` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `last_seen` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `dark_mode` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;
